Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The crystals variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 386 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 819 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 386 + O(1/n^819). Summing over all modes and applying the crystals convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 221.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: For any crystals module M satisfying the optimality condition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 373. The projective resolution has length at most 272 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.
